# SP123_RD_36 — Decisions and changes

These totals are derived from the current logged loci. One locus has one primary error-sheet category and can involve both witnesses; C and H columns are not independent observations. They are neither token-edit totals nor estimates of error rates in the archive. A doubt resolved in favor of its existing first candidate is a mark decision, not proof of an earlier wrong reading. Added uncertainty without a changed preferred reading belongs in the doubts apparatus. Audit-box counts use a separate unit and are reported in the audit table.

Pure macro normalization is excluded. H editorial synopses are not treated as source transcriptions.

## Independent image-only audit

Counts are populated only after main adjudication and integrity validation. Differing boxes and token hunks include formatting differences; changed boxes count substantive revisions arising in those differing boxes, including changed uncertainty and graphics. Additional graphic refinements prompted by independent observations where the text TSVs agree are recorded separately in the page change rows and notes. Every differing box is adjudicated, including those whose first reading stands. Full token decisions are in the page review records; exact locked inputs and comparisons are in `second/`.

Witness coverage: All rows of differences.tsv were inspected. H is an editorial synopsis explicitly declining to reproduce the leaf, so its metadata, paraphrases and mathematical summaries are excluded from source collation. There are no actual H word disagreements to crop and no H omission rows for intentionally untranscribed blocks. All C text, including agreed-looking wording, was read visually in the full scan and both native halves. All three indexed C marks have individual dispositions. Six substantive C decisions are recorded: the title's framing, the stacked-reference placement, one contiguous punctuation/cancelled-sign locus, two certainty-only resolutions, and removal of the point in p.1. Replacing text-bearing TikZ nodes with ordinary TeX, setting short circled references with textcircled, and adjusting the ruling contours are not counted as textual changes.

Remaining unresolved places:

- pg6, cancelled row: `\deleted{\illegible{5}}` — Dense crossing and hatching obscure nearly every letter body; the five-unit estimate is inherited from C and remains approximate.
- pg7, cancelled item between si and que: `\illegible{1}` — Surviving strokes cannot be separated from the crossing-out.
- p4, second initial: `\unsure{M|$\Pi$}` — A and both stops are clear. The next initial has two uprights and an almost level bridge; its shallow middle is compatible with M but does not exclude a Pi-shaped form. Local comparison no longer warrants certainty.
- pg5, symbol after classes: `$\unsure{w|\omega}$` — Two low turns and a rising right limb favour w, but the cramped outline also permits omega. The audit restores the original alphabet doubt.
- pg7, circled relation: `$\unsure{\subset|<}$` — The two arms meet at a rounded left turn; their divergence permits either an inclusion cup or less-than sign. No independent middle bar is visible.
- pg7, following dotted glyph: `$\unsure{\dot{L}_0|i_0}$` — The detached top dot and low zero are clear. The height and extended foot favour L but do not uniquely distinguish this isolated handwritten form.
- pg7, cancelled short word: `\unsure{si|ni}` — A detached i-dot is clear, but the preceding cursive entry is crossed by the zigzag and can be s or n. The earlier confident si is withdrawn.
- pg8, word below proposed pour: `\unsure{à|k}` — The compact lower glyph has an oblique upper extension and angular body; the crossing-free shape is compatible with à or k. It is not deleted.
- final reference, deleted digits before 20: `\deleted{\unsure{19|15|17}}` — The first digit is an upright 1. The second has an upper loop or angular bar and a descending right stroke, favouring 9; crossing strokes leave 5 and 7 possible. A closed lower loop supporting 6 is not discernible.

Remaining unresolved places:

- item before on a un morphisme: `\deleted{\illegible{1}}` — No underlying a) can be isolated from the dense hatching; the item is physically illegible.
- parenthesis after topos map: `\unsure{ext.|ent.}` — The middle glyph is n-like, but same-page x in faisceaux is similarly angular; both candidates remain possible.
- final line, compressed noun: `\unsure{trait|tout}` — A low r-like shoulder and narrow joined middle favour trait, but the middle strokes do not separate securely enough to exclude tout.
- pg28 bracketed supplement: `\inserted{[\unsure{itou|item} pour 3.7]}` — The dotted i and separate tall crossed t are clear. The final rounded loop and connected trough favour ou, but the compressed e/m reading remains possible.
- pg36 pencilled response: `\unsure{si} ?` — The soft, joined pencil strokes do not justify the first-pass certainty.

Witness coverage: All rows in differences.tsv align continuous C transcription against the H editorial synopsis. Those editorial phrases, source paraphrases, page-note wrapper and macro-only differences are filtered in full; there are no genuine C/H word divergences to adjudicate on this page because H supplies no continuous source transcription. Every C mark has a disposition. Every substantive departure from C, including certainty-only decisions, source punctuation, case, and contour details, is recorded above. No source block is omitted from the inventory or draft.

Remaining unresolved places:

- upper-left response: `\unsure{c'est}` — The compressed pale apostrophe/est strokes cannot all be separated.
- pg60, negation: `\unsure{pas|ps}` — The low joining return may be a compressed a or merely the p-to-s join.
- pg61, negation: `\unsure{pas|ps}` — The same compression does not independently settle the a.
- reworked page numeral: `\unsure{64|68|67}` — An upper fork, right stem and lower loop are superposed in one digit position.
- cancelled suffix after slash: `\deleted{\unsure{bis}}` — Two diagonal strokes cross the letter bodies; deletion is established but the full word is not.
- cancelled word before Serre parenthesis: `\deleted{\illegible{1}}` — The heavy hatching does not leave a complete letter sequence.
- Serre parenthesis: `\unsure{l'indép.|l'insép.}` — Oblique medial arm and low bowl favour d, but the compressed s reading remains possible.
- p91, ending before -tu: `\unsure{mettrait|mettrais}` — Tall final shaft and transverse trace favour t; rounded return also permits s.

Witness coverage: H007 is one editorial note, explicitly non reproduites, with no continuous transcription. All differences.tsv alignments to that synopsis, quotations, prose descriptions and macro code were filtered from formal H collation. The quoted marginal text was nevertheless read and cropped. The editorial page 66 and top-oval extent are discussed in notes; neither is counted as an H source-reading row. All six C marks have individual decisions: three resolved, three retained; one new certainty-only doubt is added at mettrais. The five counted C departures comprise ps to pas, three resolutions, and the omitted final period. Macro conversion from TikZ text nodes to ordinary TeX, contour geometry, and placement of marginal annotations are not counted as lexical changes. Main review additionally counts the changed physical underline scope as a graphic-reading locus; this is not a macro-only change.

Witness coverage: All differences.tsv rows were inspected. H's synopsis, its paraphrases of the mathematics and its trailing section heading are excluded from formal source collation; none is treated as a missing transcription block. The explicit marginal quotation que faire ? and the stated shared enclosure with pg 103 were checked on a crop and agree with the ink. C's sole substantive departure concerns the enclosure's scope and placement. Replacing text-bearing TikZ nodes with ordinary TeX or textcircled does not itself count as a reading change. Every C word and formula was read from the full image and native halves, including tu affirme, the abbreviation m with circumflex, and biextension. marks.tsv has no entries; no doubt was resolved, retained or newly introduced.

- Upper diagonal word: `\unsure{Situation|fibration}` — Tall stems and detached dots survive, but low middle joins are compressed.
- Isolated right upright: `\unsure{\vert|1}` — No foot, serif or connected shaft identifies its function.
- Upper T-to-G connector: `\unsure{\longrightarrow|\mathord{-}}` — Dense hooked terminal has no two separable head arms.
- Lower T-prime-to-G-prime connector: `\unsure{\longrightarrow|\mathord{-}}` — Small upturned thick loop may be head or retrace.
- Lower left endpoint: `\unsure{0|\bullet}` — Tiny open curve is not a complete zero.
- Lower terminal connector: `\unsure{\leftrightarrow|\rightarrow}` — Right head is clear; smaller left terminal angle may be a head or a starting retrace.
- Inner lower D object: `\unsure{\mu|M}` — Descending left stem, rounded valley and right return; angular upper M is graphically distinct.
- Lower free object: `\unsure{\mu\prime|M\prime}` — Same rounded formation as the inner lower D glyph, with a clear detached prime.
- Lower diagonal label: `\unsure{\beta\lambda|R\lambda}` — Two bowl regions under dense retracing; the second glyph has two lambda legs.

Witness coverage: All six C marks are individually disposed of: Situation, the isolated dash and upper filled point are resolved; the right upright, lower endpoint and overwritten lower label remain uncertain. A fourth uncertainty is added at the lower terminal arrow where both witnesses gave a right arrow. All substantive H diagram differences are included: the two left endpoints/connectors, both vertical relation renderings, both G-to-B connectors, upper terminal direction and newly uncertain lower terminal direction. The separate dash, upright and foliation absent from H receive distinct omission rows. H's accurate editorial descriptions of the diagonal arrows, M' and cross are not formal reading differences; its unread-sign description gives no letter-level reading and is recorded only as context. Page markers, ampersand replacement, row/column spacing, macro wrappers and token-diff fragmentation are excluded. There is no actual C/H lexical disagreement in Situation, only shared uncertainty resolved by the crop.

Remaining unresolved places:

- R00543: `\unsure{M}` — Dense cancellation obscures the central argument; M remains the candidate, not a resolved reading.
- R00544: `\unsure{\mathbb G_m}` — The second argument is densely overwritten; the supplied G_m reading cannot be settled letter by letter.
- R00545: `\unsure{)}` — The closing region meets the dark scan edge; the outer closing parenthesis is not securely separable.
- R00546: `\unsure{)}` — The right edge clips the closing region; retain the doubtful outer parenthesis.
- R00554: `\unsure{Exemp}` — No separate l/e ending is exposed after the p descent.
- R00555: `\illegible{1}` — Dense local loops and crossings do not expose par letter by letter.
- R00556: `\illegible{1}` — The small crossed item cannot support a complete était.
- R00557: `\unsure{est}` — The raised strokes permit est, but their compressed conjunction with the cancellation is not decisive.
- R00558: `\unsure{rev\textsuperscript{t}|vers} : \unsure{à} \unsure{donner|donné}` — Contracted first group, separate colon, hooked middle sign and compressed last word prevent a complete reading; no se is added.
- R00560: `\unsure{réciproq\textsuperscript{t}}` — Long crossed handwritten word has r/c/p-like strokes and a raised terminal trace; the candidate remains uncertain.
- R00561: `\unsure{\operatorname{Ext}}` — Retracing joins the E/x/t strokes and prevents certainty.
- R00563: `\unsure{aussi|ainsi}` — Flattened middle joins allow the independent ainsi reading; aussi remains preferred.
- R00565: `\unsure{équiv} :` — The group is short and followed by two distinct dots, not the longer équivaut à.
- R00566: `\unsure{splittages|splittage}` — The g descent is visible, but a separate final s is not secure.
- R00568: `\unsure{ou aux|ou à}` — Initial o/u is visible; no tall h permits hom.
- R00569: `\unsure{dans le|sur $k$}` — The strike obscures both low groups; dans le is preferred while sur k remains possible.
- R00570: `\unsure{Si}` — Tall S-like entry and detached dot survive the local strike.
- R00571: `\unsure{se}` — The small word after j' is crossed locally; its surviving e-like loop does not settle the whole word.
- R00573: `\unsure{les|des} \unsure{conditions}` — Tall first loop, low es, central d ascender and late dot support new tentative candidates under the crossing.
- R00574: `\illegible{1}` — Nested crossed mass exposes no separable sigma/prime.
- R00575: `\unsure{donné}` — The baseline of the candidate donné is crossed by the lower rule; retain uncertainty despite surviving upper strokes.
- R00576: `\unsure{et} de` — Pale first group is not fully separated from show-through; de is clear.
- blind audit B05: `\unsure{une} biext.\inserted{$\mathcal E$} sous $\mathbb G_m$` — Final root adjudication B05: Qualify the compressed opening; retain the distinct doubled G and the E caret.
- blind audit B17: `un isom, \unsure{alors} $i'$` — Final root adjudication B17: The middle word has a round initial and tall l but flattened final joins.
- blind audit B24: `\unsure{aussi|ainsi} pour. \unsure{Donc}` — Final root adjudication B24: Aussi/ainsi remains open; pour is readable and the capital-D group is qualified.
- blind audit B27: `l'ext. $G$. Et \unsure{réciproquement}` — Final root adjudication B27: The long final word remains qualified and lacks a separate full stop.
- blind audit B28: `\inserted{$\mathcal E_{\unsure{i'|i},i'}$} de $\mathcal E$` — Final root adjudication B28: An open balloon leader holds a restriction whose first i has a possible prime.
- blind audit B36: `\deleted{\unsure{Si}} \inserted{Deux \unsure{relèv\textsuperscript{ts}|relèvements}} $j$ et $j'$ \deleted{\unsure{se}}` — Final root adjudication B36: The upper replacement begins Deux and ends with a contracted relèv-ts candidate.
- blind audit B44: `\deleted{ces \unsure{deux} splitt. $\sigma$}` — Final root adjudication B44: Qualify deux but resolve the separated splitt.-sigma expression.
- blind audit B49: `$\mathcal E_{M,M'}$\unsure{)|,}` — Final root adjudication B49: The tall curved final mark favours a closing parenthesis but remains ambiguous.
- blind audit B50: `\unsure{les|l'un} \unsure{déduits|déduit} d'\unsure{une|eux}` — Final root adjudication B50: Restore the short initial group and qualify the compressed following words.
- blind audit B54: `\unsure{du|un} splittage $\rho'$ de $\mathcal E_{\unsure{G|M},\unsure{M'|G'}}$` — Final root adjudication B54: The initial du/un is open, and the complete restriction is retained from this and B55.
- blind audit B55: `$\mathcal E_{\unsure{G|M},\unsure{M'|G'}}$ et de` — Final root adjudication B55: Both retraced index bodies remain explicit alternatives.

Witness coverage: Every substantive C/H reading divergence in the supplied difference list is covered by a local change row, including absent Ext degrees, arrow forms, vertical versus horizontal relations, the example wording, the correspondence conditions, inserted labels, live versus cancelled layers, all recoverable elements of H's long illegible span, and the final j/j' maps. Macro-only TikZ/array encodings, whitespace, purely typographic font packaging, and H's editorial \note prose/page marker are filtered. C doubt decisions are all listed. The two clipped top-right closing parentheses, obscure local cancellations, contracted heading, faint connective, alternative hom phrase and ambiguous cancelled splitting expression remain open. No source text is inferred from an editorial synopsis or from mathematical expectation. Main review excludes the accurate editorial descriptions of the two lower identifications, and the non-reading of the cancelled word, from formal H counts. These elements remain independently checked and included in inventory/audit.

Remaining unresolved places:

- R00578: `\unsure{conséquence} de` — The d ascender/bowl and e are exposed; only the preceding word remains masked.
- R00580: `\illegible{1}` — Dense strokes conceal the whole short fragment; et is withdrawn.
- R00583: `\unsure{4|2}` — Crowded upper and lower layers permit 4 or 2; the following punctuation is a point.
- R00584: `\phi^n(\unsure{mx},y)=\phi^n(m^2x',y)=\phi^n` — The formula outside the first mx can be read: three phi/n signs, both equality pairs, y, m-squared and x-prime. The first argument remains under the wavy strike.
- R00585: `\unsure{homomorphisme|hmomorphisme}` — Closed oval visible beside h, but initial typewriter-cell overlap does not establish whether it is an additional o or a replacement layer. Preferred candidate reversed, uncertainty retained.
- R00586: `\unsure{\partial|\delta}` — Sweeping top arc and small open lower loop favour partial, but do not exclude a loosely written delta. Preferred candidate reversed.
- R00588: `\unsure{4|\xi}` — Angular upper turn and upright lower component favour 4, with xi still possible under cancellation.
- R00589: `\unsure{\xi}` — Tiny multi-stroke mark has a xi-like right contour, crossed by oblique strokes; original glyph not fully recoverable.
- middle coefficient in surviving equality: `\unsure{m|n}` — An n-like outer arch and heavily darkened/additional inner arch occupy one cell. m remains preferred; the order of the overtype layers is not established. This adds uncertainty only and does not count as a substantive departure from C.
- underlying glyph in initial cell of dans: `\deleted{\illegible{1}}dans` — The live d is clear; the low hook does not recover a complete underlying character. No q, ç, or comma is supplied as a resolved prior glyph.
- blind audit B04: `\unsure{est}` — The e is concealed by typed X and an ink strike; s and t survive. The replacement implique is fully legible.
- blind audit B20: `\deleted{\unsure{c}}Comme` — An earlier c-like curve is visible but partly merged into the reinforced capital C.
- R00579: `\deleted{\unsure{3|5}}4` — Clear live 4 above a rounded lower layer admitting 3 or 5; no secure 1.

Witness coverage: H030 is an editorial synopsis expressly non reproduit, not a continuous transcription. No formal H source-reading rows or omission rows were generated. Its quoted additions and cancelled-word claim were checked against the scan; the donc/dans discrepancy is noted with its crop, not counted as H collation. All 13 C marks have decisions: four resolved, nine retained (two with preferred candidate order reversed). Nine substantive C loci are recorded; the new m|n uncertainty is a doubts-only addition. Graphic/text placement of the isolated et-like deletion outside TikZ is not a lexical change.

Remaining unresolved places:

- Cancelled biextension ending: `\unsure{biextension}` — The initial letters and continuation beyond t are visible; the struck ending is not completely recoverable.
- Oblique marginal question: `\unsure{pour ainsi dire|pourquoi}` — Three spaced groups and a final d-like ascender favor the multiword reading. The separate question mark is clear.
- Cancelled letter before Ext: `\unsure{R}` — Overstriking leaves only an R-like outline before Ext.
- Struck word before nB=B: `\unsure{par}` — The first p-like stroke is visible; the rest is obscured. This struck word is separate from live nB=B.
- Interpretation abbreviation: `\unsure{interprét\textsuperscript{tion}|interpr.\textsuperscript{tion}}` — The boundary between baseline ét and the raised ending cannot be separated securely. Written short forms remain unexpanded.
- Cancelled word beneath canonique: `\unsure{de|des}` — The cancellation is definite, but the terminal curl may include s.
- Outward-left connector beside M: `\unsure{\leftarrow|\mathbin{-}}` — An angled tip may be a head or a pen flick.
- Group letter in second exact sequence: `\unsure{G'|C'}` — An open curve lacks a secure G bar. Local notation ranks G' without settling the ink.
- blind audit B07: `\deleted{\unsure{p\textsuperscript{r}|ps} \unsure{dire|dis} qu'on a}` — The two crossed groups retain only the short forms and letter alternatives supported by exposed strokes.
- blind audit B09: `\unsure{<|-}` — The converging degree sign is not normalized to the expected minus.
- blind audit B10: `\unsure{\longrightarrow|\mathbin{-}}` — The upper-right barb is pale.
- blind audit B13: `\unsure{complexe} de\par\unsure{faisceaux|chaînes} ?` — Compressed first word and long lower word retain the stated alternatives; ? is resolved.
- blind audit B15: `on \unsure{va|en} \unsure{définir|définit} une` — Low va/en and final r/t remain locally uncertain.
- blind audit B22: `\unsure{Soit|Soient}` — Live word, uncertain t/ent ending.
- blind audit B23: `\unsure{dfn|def}` — No secure accent and an n-like last letter.
- blind audit B25: `\unsure{que|qu'on}` — Possible apostrophe within the low q-run.
- blind audit B27: `\unsure{triangle|triangles}` — No independently separable final s.
- blind audit B28: `\unsure{Soit}` — Cancellation crosses the broad middle letters.
- blind audit B29: `\unsure{resp.|pq}` — Balloon and leader overlap the letters.
- blind audit B34: `(D(\unsure{M'_n|M_n}),\unsure{nB|{}_nB}),\,\text{si }nT=T` — Prime and placement of the n beside B remain uncertain under the overlying contour.
- blind audit B39: `\unsure{passant|passons}` — The long low final run does not securely distinguish ant/ons.
- blind audit B40: `\unsure{on a|on les}` — The last tall loop and low return are compressed.

Witness coverage: All 22 C marks have dispositions: 13 resolved and 9 retained, including narrowed word/state doubts. Seven new local doubts are recorded, including a separately disposed new doubt inside the newly recognized cancelled de/des. Every substantive H source-reading difference is covered, including readable opening/prose, deleted words, degree punctuation, complex connector/punctuation, diagram directions/shafts/outward strokes, class-group alphabet, marginal reading, integer typography resolutions where relevant, conditions and parenthesis, marginal abbreviation/n position, transition omission, Tate normalization/arrows, and foliation. H's actual transcription is collated. Its literal quoted degree/caption/cancelled-formula readings are distinguished from editorial description. Correct descriptions of circles, braces, degree placement and the underbrace are acknowledged rather than manufactured into omissions; descriptive prose supplies no invented source words. The code-only changes that move running text out of TikZ nodes, replace macro aliases, change matrix spacing or repair display wrappers are excluded as separate collation rows. The uncertain arrowheads remain uncertainties, not proven directional corrections.

- First cancelled flank in (2.2.4): `\deleted{\unsure{\$}}` — Dense pen overwriting.
- Second cancelled flank in (2.2.4): `\deleted{\unsure{\$}}` — Dense pen overwriting.

- Lower-left object in (2.3.1): `{}_{\deleted{\unsure{m|n}}n}P` — Right arch exposed but left part hidden by the cancellation.

Witness coverage: H is an editorial synopsis with no continuous source transcription. Every quoted intervention was inspected in ink and discussed in notes; no formal source rows are manufactured for synopsis omissions or embedded editorial quotations. All 18 C departure loci, all seven supplied doubt marks, and the two newly observed uncertain overtypes are represented. Fifteen xi additions are grouped in ten precisely located change entries. TeX-only differences are excluded.

Remaining unresolved places:

- R00619: `\unsure{Les inclusions}` — Initial L and a few raised strokes survive, but the long strike suppresses several letters.
- R00620: `\unsure{des hom}` — The d loop and low es favors des; the following faint h/om is incomplete and no end stop is secure.
- R00621: `\unsure{'}` — A possible pale raised mark after the second A cannot be separated securely from reverse typing. The whole Ext object was inspected.
- R00622: `\unsure{\mathbin{-}|=}0` — A single bar and terminal closed loop are visible; no arrowhead. A faint second bar cannot be confirmed.
- R00624: `\unsure{bien} définis \unsure{glob\textsuperscript{t}}\unsure{.}` — Short pale first group favors bien; terminal s is distinct in définis. Compressed glob with raised t remains uncertain.
- R00627: `\unsure{M'_n|M_n}` — Several retraced M strokes crowd the low n and a raised stroke; the latter may be a prime but its separation is insecure.
- R00628: `\unsure{et}` — Multiple oblique cancellation strokes cover the interior of the low loop and tall final stroke.
- R00629: `D(M_{\unsure{n}}` — D, opening parenthesis and M are exposed; only the low ending is masked. No closing parenthesis is supplied.
- R00630: `\unsure{{}_nA/{}_n}` — An additional low n-like fragment stands after the slash; crossed parts of the initial formula remain obscure.
- R00632: `\unsure{et}` — Several slanting strokes hide the middle of the low group and tall stem.
- R00633: `\unsure{{}_nT_n|{}_nT}` — A T top bar and hooked stem survive; low crossed fragments favor indices, not the prose word et.
- R00634: `\unsure{\alpha|n}T_n` — The first looped prefix is at baseline, whereas the final n is distinctly lower; alpha/n shape remains uncertain.
- R00635: `\unsure{n|\alpha}T'_n` — The prefix sits beside T at body height. The separate T prime and low right n are clear; n/alpha prefix remains unresolved.
- R00636: `\unsure{donc}` — Opening d survives; the middle and end are buried by the strike.
- first induced pairing lower torus label: `{}_n\unsure{T'|T}` — The touching upright favors a prime; the alternative bar-end turn is retained.
- upper-diagram diagonal endpoint: `\arrow[dr,start anchor=south east,end anchor=west,shorten >=5pt,bend right=6,
dash pattern=on 7mm off 3mm on 40mm]
\draw[gray,line width=0.3pt]
([xshift=-5pt,yshift=4pt]\tikzcdmatrixname-2-2.west)
-- ([xshift=-1pt,yshift=-1pt]\tikzcdmatrixname-2-2.west)
-- ([xshift=-7pt,yshift=-4pt]\tikzcdmatrixname-2-2.west);` — Two endpoint strokes of unequal darkness are visible. Their physical outlines are reproduced without selecting an algebraic interpretation.
- initial map endpoints: `{}_nA\unsure{\longrightarrow|\mathbin{-}} A,\qquad{}_nA'\unsure{\longrightarrow|\mathbin{-}} A'` — The thin shafts end in heavy downturned or short upright tips; a full two-arm head is not secure.
- prose class symbol and abbreviation: `l'\unsure{élémt} $\unsure{\xi|\zeta}$` — The subject is compressed to élémt; the Greek curl/descender admits xi or zeta.
- pairing class symbol and endpoint: `\varphi_n^{\unsure{\xi|\zeta}}:{}_nA\otimes{}_nA'\unsure{\longrightarrow|\mathbin{-}} G_m` — The pairing letter is varphi, but its superscript admits xi/zeta and the right tip admits a headless stroke.
- first quotient vertical identification: `\unsure{\wr|\rho}` — Single upright hooked stroke may be an isomorphism squiggle or rho.
- second quotient vertical identification: `\unsure{\wr|\rho}` — Single upright hook has an open loop and no separately visible second stroke.
- third pairing marked symbols: `\deleted{\unsure{et}}\quad \begin{tikzcd}[ampersand replacement=\&]{}_nG/{}_n\unsure{T|\Pi}\&\times\&{}_nG'/{}_nT'\\\unsure{\Vert|\wr}\ \deleted{\unsure{{}_nT_n|{}_nT}}\&\&\unsure{\rho|\wr}\\{}_n\unsure{\underline{B}|B}\&\&{}_nB'\end{tikzcd}` — Denominator T is heavily retraced, left relation has a joined upper curve, lower B has a separate-looking base stroke, and right relation has a closed rho-like loop. Alternatives remain explicit.
- corollary opening parenthesis: `\unsure{car|(car}` — Large c-shaped curve joins the following ar; a separate opening parenthesis is not independently secure.
- corollary final B-prime prefix: `nB=B,\quad \unsure{n|\mathbin{-}}B'=B'` — The second baseline prefix is nearly flat, allowing n or a dash.
- corollary second zero: `{}_nM=0,\quad{}_nM'=\unsure{0}` — Second zero is a filled small diamond; both n prefixes remain visibly lower-left.

Witness coverage: All substantive wording, deletion, symbol, punctuation and graphic differences were collated. H illegibles and genuine omissions are local rows, including the corollary rule and foliation. Macro spelling, display spacing, order of index syntax, H emphasis for underlines and blackboard wrappers on G_m are excluded as normalization. Accurate lower-label descriptions in H notes are not omissions. The revised abbreviated R00624 phrase is recorded as a C/H departure. The first torus-label reading now prefers T-prime with T retained: a C departure, while the preferred source characters agree with H. Retained marks with the same preferred source characters as H are in decisions/doubts, not manufactured change rows.

Remaining unresolved places:

- cancelled phrase after un before accouplement bilinéaire: `\unsure{forme homomorphisme}` — Typed x overstrikes merge with several internal letter contours; grammar is not used to complete them.
- cancelled token after 2.2. Soit: `\unsure{ni|n'}` — The old second cell has a dot at i height, below the separate live prime; ni is preferred but overstrike prevents excluding n-prime.
- upper middle object of (2.2.2), cancelled left subscript: `\unsure{n}` — The small arch is visible but the lower strokes are covered.
- upper middle object of (2.2.2), cancelled trailing group: `\unsure{Q}` — The possible outer bowl and tail cannot both be followed through the cancellation.
- lower left object of (2.2.2), cancelled initial group: `\unsure{P|Q}` — The heavy cancelled capital has an upper bowl but its lower contour is obscured; P and Q remain possible.
- separate cancelled cell after que before l'on ait: `\illegible{1}` — The separate cancelled cell has short thick obliques but no recoverable letter contour; neither grammar nor the adjacent live e identifies it.

Witness coverage: All rows in differences.tsv were inspected. They align C's continuous source transcription against H's editorial synopsis and its following adjacent-material heading/note. They therefore create no formal H source readings or omitted-block rows. Every quoted claim concerning ink on this page was checked visually and discussed in notes where needed. All substantive departures from C, including source layers and the newly retained old reference digit, are recorded in changes; encoding-only repairs are excluded. All seven supplied C marks have individual dispositions: R00642 is resolved as \times; the other six remain undecided.

Remaining unresolved places:

- upper-middle opening prose: `\unsure{élémts|élts}` — Small e-like curve, tall l, two high dots and compact final stroke run support an abbreviation, with exact internal minims uncertain.
- upper-middle first Hom argument: `\unsure{U|\zeta}_\ell` — Two U-like uprights are crossed by angular zeta-like strokes; ell is independently clear. No earlier layer is recoverable.
- upper-middle second Hom argument: `\unsure{U|\zeta}_\ell` — Retraced capital has two upright sides and internal angular crossings; base U/zeta remains uncertain, ell is clear.
- upper-middle words after pairing: `\unsure{en vertu}` — Two word groups are present, but most of each lies close to the baseline.
- middle-middle first prose word: `\unsure{élémts|élts}` — Compact initial accented e/l/e trace and a low run favor élémts, with élts possible; no expansion.
- middle-middle second formula, first prose word: `\unsure{élémts|élts}` — Small initial curve and tall l precede dotted short strokes and a long compressed ending, favoring an abbreviation.
- lower-middle coefficient: `\unsure{i}_p` — Dot and p are separate and clear; the base letter has overlapping loops and remains qualified as i.
- lower-middle elevated cancelled mark: `\begin{tikzpicture}[x=.25ex,y=.25ex]\fill (0,3)--(1.2,4.2)--(2.8,5.1)--(4.2,5)--(4.8,4.6)--(6.4,5.3)--(6.5,4.6)--(5.6,3.8)--(5.9,2.8)--(5.2,1.5)--(3.7,0)--(3.3,.8)--(2.8,.1)--(2.2,.8)--(1.1,.7)--(1.2,2.7)--cycle;\end{tikzpicture}` — Dense irregular crossed patch has no independently recoverable F or other letter. The patch is drawn, its concealed value unresolved.
- upper-left retraced middle factor: `\unsure{\mathbb Z|\zeta}_\ell` — Retraced double diagonal and flat bars favor Z, but zeta remains possible. Ell is separately lowered.
- point after X in upper-middle prose: `\unsure{,|.}` — The detached low mark is visible but its small tail is not secure.

Witness coverage: All substantive C/H word, symbol, deletion, placement and graphic differences were read against the page. H has continuous source transcription and accurate notes for the upper-left cancellation, the upper-middle cancelled expression, both frames and the brace; those descriptive placements are not manufactured omissions. Its quoted cancelled expression does supply an absent closing parenthesis and omit the ditto strokes. Its quoted marginal et is a genuine disputed reading and is collated against the separate source marks. Editorial row headings, linearization punctuation, layout commands, handwriting wrappers and the explicitly declared fraktur-z/U convention are excluded. The uniform calligraphic O wrapper is treated as normalization by inference from H usage, with the source plain O retained. The detached mark after X remains \unsure{,|.}; H’s omitted plural s in annulés supplies the substantive discrepancy at that locus. H omits the elevated lower-middle cancellation and pencil35. The three \unsure{élémts|élts} abbreviations, en vertu, the two \unsure{U|\zeta}_\ell arguments and the middle \unsure{i}_p remain qualified according to the sealed readings. The preferred abbreviation now differs from C at all three openings: two Points readings and one élts reading give way to élémts first, with élts retained as an alternative. The sign after P^{(p)} is resolved as = against the witnesses’ colon; its C/H row records that sign change.

Remaining unresolved places:

- isolated marks: `\begin{tikzpicture}[x=1mm,y=1mm] \fill (0,4) ellipse (.12 and .17); \draw[line width=.3pt] (-.2,0) .. controls (.5,.05) and (.8,-.5) .. (.8,-1.2); \end{tikzpicture}` — A compact dot and a separate short right-curving hook are visible; their intended function remains unassigned.
- critere: `\unsure{rationalité}` — Short initial upright/return, a tall crossed t and dotted compressed middle favor rationalité, without securely separating every letter.
- fibre universel: `\unsure{Fibrés universels|Fibré universel}` — Large F, looped b and low terminal returns favor the plural phrase, while singular remains possible.
- voir pic: `\unsure{Pic|Ric}` — The capital has a top loop and lower diagonal return, permitting P/R; i dot and final c are visible. No separate abbreviation stop precedes the three strokes.
- projectivite: `\unsure{projectivité}` — Descending initial group and tall/dotted internal traces favor projectivité, but several letters are compressed.
- finicielles: `\unsure{fonctorielles|finicielles}` — Looped f and the joined low middle favor fonctorielles; compressed interior strokes do not eliminate finicielles.
- gpes fibres: `\unsure{gpes|groupes}` — The g/p-like forms are joined by a flat run that could either be an abbreviation or conceal the medial letters rou. The short form remains first.
- leur dualite: `\unsure{Sans} doute` — The short first group favors Sans rather than two words; the following d loop and crossed t favor doute.
- leur dualite: `\unsure{par|pour}` — The long p-like descender and low run leave the middle vowel and ending unresolved.
- representations: `\unsure{représentation|organisation} \unsure{lin.} de \unsure{gpes|groupes}` — The long descending stem and crossed t favor singular représentation, with organisation possible. Lin. and the gpes/groupes ending remain compressed.
- integer map: `\mathrel{\begin{tikzpicture}[x=1ex,y=1ex,baseline=.3ex]
\draw (.38,1.45) .. controls (.21,1.08) and (.03,.28) .. (.04,.02)
 .. controls (.08,-.18) and (.19,.22) .. (.34,.34)
 .. controls (.55,.21) and (.95,.3) .. (1.15,.34)
 .. controls (1.36,.4) and (1.56,.31) .. (1.66,.18);
\draw[line width=.6pt] (.35,.35) .. controls (.76,.2) and (1.15,.43) .. (1.53,.27);
\end{tikzpicture}}` — A tall curved left stroke joins a broad retraced lower right arm with a blunt terminal; the contour is preserved and no intended connector symbol is assigned.
- Article before V.A.: `\unsure{des|du}` — The d loop is followed by a compressed low run; a final s is not separately secure. Des remains the preferred C reading, and du is retained as H's possible alternative.
- Opening degrees: `H^{\unsure{1|n}},\ \underline{\operatorname{Ext}}^{\unsure{1|n}}` — Both separate high angular marks favor 1 but can resemble n. An elevated mark after Ext is present.
- Central left argument: `\unsure{f^*(B)|f_!(B)}` — A high angular mark and low point lie next to the long f; star versus low exclamation remains unresolved.
- Right Ext base: `\unsure{S|s}` — The small hooked base is below Ext; its capital/lowercase size is not secure.
- First item endings: `\unsure{Suites|Suite} \unsure{exactes|exacte} de` — Low hooked returns can be plural s or terminal e strokes; plural is preferred without resolving the alternatives.
- Cartier: `\unsure{Cartier}` — The name is highly compressed into a capital C and a tall crossed stroke with a flat ending.
- voir: `\unsure{voir}` — The reduced v-like opening and flat terminal do not separate all letters.
- rigidité: `\unsure{rigidité}` — The r, dotted i, looped g and t are visible but the intervening letters remain compressed.
- Fifth-item noun: `\unsure{groupes}` — The g and descending p-like group are present but the medial strokes are flattened.
- Seventh-item adjective: `\unsure{particuliers}` — The long p-like initial and tall/dotted internal strokes favor the plural word; its ending remains compressed.

Witness coverage: Every actual reading difference in the supplied C/H comparison was considered against the scan. H's systematic G/mathbb-G wrappers, spacing, macro syntax, itemization and editorial descriptions are not independent source readings or correction rows. Its accurate descriptions of the underbrace, raised A, upward double shaft, horizontal separators, list contour, double underline and possible bottom cancellation are recorded without invented omissions. Substantive lexical, number/ending, index-position and punctuation differences are collated, along with the omitted marginal marks and foliation. All eleven original C marks remain qualified; the Pic mark loses an unsupported stop, the gpes mark adds a permitted full-form alternative, and the isolated-mark layout is encoded vertically. The new des|du doubt leaves C's preferred reading unchanged and is not a C correction. The separate baseline point after différentielles is retained without qualification following the audit.

Remaining unresolved places:

- Opening bracket: first word: `\unsure{généraliser|généralisons}` — A g-like initial, tall medial l and compact terminal run favor généraliser; généralisons remains possible, with no tall b supporting généralisable.
- Cancelled sign after first limit equality: `\begin{tikzpicture}[x=1ex,y=1ex,baseline=0pt]
\fill (0,.35)--(.03,.53)--(.23,.59)--(.27,.72)--(.41,.66)--(.48,.7)--(.55,.62)--(.78,.58)--(.92,.46)--(.95,.23)--(.87,.25)--(.84,.36)--(.64,.38)--(.35,.36)--cycle;
\fill (.14,.04) ellipse (.055 and .05);
\end{tikzpicture}` — The irregular dense patch conceals the upper sign; a separate low dot survives. Draw both without recovering an unsupported underlying sign.
- Crossed sign before H^1 in the first exact sequence: `\unsure{n}` — The crossed arch-like sign stands below-left of H at subscript height. Its n-like remnant remains uncertain and deleted.
- Sequence (*), exponent on first Pic: `\unsure{0|\tau}` — Dense nearly filled high mark has a rounded body and protruding cap;0 and tau remain alternatives.
- Raised inserted word before sur n: `\unsure{inductive}` — The raised word has an initial upright/dot, compressed low groups, a central retraced tall stroke and later separate dot, then short terminal turns. Inductive fits the visible sequence, but several letters remain compressed. Its leader attaches before sur n.
- Final word of the application line: `\unsure{trouve}` — The first tall stroke and flat joined middle followed by two low terminal turns favor trouve; the individual letters are not all separately recoverable.
- Sequence (**), exponent on Pic in the Ext term: `\unsure{\tau|2}` — The superscript consists of a dark cap, slanting descent and hooked lower end, permitting τ or 2 and not a securely closed 0. τ remains first after the main reader’s supplied related-notation comparison, without eliminating 2.
- Transition before duality: `\unsure{en effet}` — A rounded first group and a long-entry second group with one tall stroke favor en effet; exact internal letters remain compressed.
- Detached sign above duality: `\unsure{l|1}` — Detached upright bends right at its foot; no separate dot or leader establishes its function.
- First outlined sequence, exponent on first Pic: `\unsure{0|\tau}` — The dense high mark has a rounded low body and projecting upper cap; its interior is not secure.
- First n in starred middle Hom: `\unsure{n}` — The lower stroke is almost flat with a small curved beginning; n remains qualified.
- First angular capital in short exact row: `\unsure{A|\Lambda}` — Two angular legs and a prime are visible, but an internal bar is not secure.
- Cancelled Ext degree: `\deleted{\operatorname{Ext}^{\unsure{1}}}` — The high stroke after t may be1; cancellation prevents a secure degree reading.
- Second sign in lower formula label: `*\unsure{*|+}` — The first mark has several rays; the second is mostly a bar and upright with weak diagonal traces.
- First n in lower labelled Hom: `\unsure{n}` — The low sign has an initial bend followed by a flat run; n is possible but not fully separated.
- Target at lower physical edge: `\unsure{0}` — Only the rounded left portion survives at the physical edge below the descending arrow; the right contour is clipped.

Witness coverage: H is an actual transcription on this page. Every substantive word/sign/connector/underline/placement departure is collated, including C retained against H and the source hook omitted by both. H page/display wrappers, tikz-cd syntax, spacing, equivalent subscript/superscript order, identical preferred readings with certainty wrappers alone, and correct editorial descriptions are filtered. The comma used to join H’s two duality displays is separately identified as absent source punctuation. H’s note acknowledging the pre-H^1 crossed sign is recorded without inventing a new H letter reading. H’s notes about the raised insertion, retraced first-outline exponent and edge contact are acknowledged, but do not erase visible gaps in the outlines, the middle Pic underline in (**), or the downward terminal connection. The first-outline connector before H1 is resolved as a right arrow; the Ext-term Pic exponent remains qualified tau/2.

Remaining unresolved places:

- Marginal ninth line: `\unsure{doit} tenir \unsure{si|de}` — Initial retracing leaves doit qualified, while the t and low enir run are recoverable. The final short group does not separate si from de. No cancellation layer is supplied.
- Lower diagram, second vertical label: `\unsure{\wr}` — The top and lower lobes are heavily darkened and the lower return forms a small closed loop.
- Conclusion, number word: `\unsure{trois}` — The short initial upright and shoulder favor tr; a d loop and terminal x crossing are not visible. The flat middle and absent distinct i dot prevent full resolution.
- Qualification after second display: `\unsure{affine|ffine}` — The two tall f stems and ine ending are visible, but a separate initial a is not. It may be absorbed into the entry stroke.

Witness coverage: All substantive C/H differences were inspected: opening Donc, abbreviation punctuation, first connector, comparison clause, affine/fini, marginal condition and illegible wording, final marginal punctuation, On/on and hom/homs, both vertical labels, conclusion wording and number, en question/une partie, and the omitted pencil foliation. C doubts R00686–R00691 all have individual dispositions. H blackboard G, prime-bracing, emph/uline, row spacing, ampersand syntax and uncertainty wrappers alone are filtered as normalization or macro differences. H descriptive prose is not treated as a missing source block. Its double-half-arrow claim is a genuine graphic discrepancy; its nine-line count is noted editorially. Unchanged preferred readings with remaining uncertainty are recorded in doubts, not manufactured C change rows. The independent audit agrees with H on qu’on comparera and changes C to voit and qualified trois; it removes certainty-only C rows and records the physical underline extent separately from resolving donc. Both witnesses normalize the absent acute in abelienne.

Remaining unresolved places:

- Isolated label above the small arrangement: `\unsure{P_X|p_X|\rho_X}` — The large high bowl and long loop-foot stem do not settle alphabet or case without a shared baseline comparator. The lower X is distinct.
- Cancelled upper-right entry of the small arrangement: `\deleted{\unsure{\gamma_X\times\gamma_X}}` — Lower loops cannot be reliably separated into original characters and cancelling or superposed strokes. No additional word has been invented from them.
- Cancelled expression at the left of the final frame: `\deleted{\unsure{\operatorname{Ext}(A,B)|\operatorname{Ext}(X,B)}}` — The main-line angular capital favors A but X remains possible beneath a crossing. B-like bowls and a low closing curve favor arguments; the cancellation leaves the whole expression qualified.
- Cancelled word in 'isom de foncteurs, ... quand': `\deleted{\unsure{pour}}` — The p-like descender and r-like ending are visible; the middle joins are masked. This newly marks uncertainty that C omitted.

Witness coverage: C and H were read against every visible source line, sign, index and punctuation. Substantive loci are reported in the changes log. The five original C marks have separate dispositions: two resolved and three retained. The new uncertainty on cancelled pour is explicit. H has continuous formal source transcription plus editorial notes. Its descriptions of the isolated label, small table and upper-left arrow are treated only as editorial descriptions; no formal H omission or variant rows are manufactured there. Its accurate blank-column and unfinished-expression notes are acknowledged without omission charges. Four supplied cohomology bars follow H’s declared editorial convention and are excluded from substantive corrections; the extra right-Hom bar remains a literal source difference. Font/macro spellings such as S-gr versus S\text{-gr}, \to versus \longrightarrow where the same simple arrow is visible, \emph{affine} versus \uline{affine}, diagram syntax, note wrappers and \end{document} are filtered. Source geometry discrepancies in the actual frames and final arrow position are retained, not confused with wrapper changes. The lower-left foliation 40 is a source number absent from H's formal transcription.

Remaining unresolved places:

- older diagram patch: `unidentified patch reproduced as strokes` — Dense zigzags and loops cannot be assigned to an original g; the separate live g remains visible.
- diagram j connector: `\unsure{\rightarrow|\mathbin{-}}` — The tip is swollen with only partly separable branches; the visible direction is retained as preferred.
- opening noun: `\unsure{préschémas}` — Descending p and tall ch-like loop support the word, but the joined letters are compressed and crossed by a global stroke.
- bracketed clause: `\unsure{alors}` — A high l-like arch precedes a flat final run; several small letters are not separable.
- complement noun: `\unsure{complémentaire}` — The c-like beginning, p descender and accent survive, while much of the long middle is flattened.
- complement abbreviation: `\unsure{ens}` — The low e/n-shaped arches and flat end favor ens; no stop is visible.
- cancelled clause: `\deleted{i.e. \unsure{ici|aussi} le \unsure{ft|$f$}}` — The horizontal strike merges with the lower bodies; i.e. and le are distinct.
- live short group: `\unsure{$f^{-}$|qu'}` — Looped upright and rightward cross-stroke favor f; the short upper stroke is visible without a numeral1.
- word before un voisinage: `\unsure{expr|existe}` — The separate e-like loop, descending central stroke and final upcurl do not settle the flattened middle.
- verb before un isom: `\unsure{induise}` — A dotted initial i, low run and short raised central stroke favor induise, with compressed middle letters.
- abbreviation before formula: `\unsure{isom}` — The extra low arch and return after the i/s/o group support m, but the whole word remains compressed.
- terminal connector: `$f^{-1}(U)\unsure{\rightarrow|\mathbin{-}}V$.` — The tip has an upper swelling and lower angle, favoring an arrow, without completely distinct branches.
- divisor symbol: `\unsure{D|\Delta}` — The right bowl and low base favor D, but the angular upper start admits Delta.
- lower parenthesis: `($X$ \unsure{connexe|normal})` — The first X is clear; the word has a c-like opening and compressed arches ending in a taller return.
- short cancellation: `\deleted{\illegible{1}}` — No separately assignable letters survive the dense obliteration.
- pullback argument: `$g^{-1}(\unsure{\eta|\xi})\in\operatorname{Pic}(U)$` — A high loop, left low turn and right descender favor eta; this occurrence is read independently of the opening symbol.

Witness coverage: Every substantive source word and graphic difference was assessed on the scan, prepared halves and 37 first-pass tight crops, then reviewed through the sealed independent audit. Macro-only deleted/struck, inserted/add, underline/emph, operator and geq/geqslant distinctions are filtered. Correct H descriptions are not omissions. Its factual two-stroke cancellation, authorial 17, live-g target and locally struck cycle claims remain accounted for. The cancelled clause now retains ici/aussi and ft/f; the live restart reads on sait with f-minus/qu’ and expr/existe qualified. The old diagram patch has no recovered letter, the short cancelled word after considérons remains illegible, and the final un is locally live within the globally deleted block. The later eta/xi argument is read independently of the opening xi. Both j and the connector before V prefer an arrow but remain qualified. Unchanged preferred readings with added uncertainty belong in doubts rather than new correction rows. H’s correct divisoriel and correspond candidates do not generate word corrections from confidence wrappers alone. R00698 remains undecided despite partial recovery.

Remaining unresolved places:

- terminal number of top annotation: `\unsure{pt singuliers|pt singulier}` — The terminal low return cannot be securely separated from a pen flourish.
- low f sign: `\unsure{!|\cdot}` — The small upper slant partly merges with the f cross-stroke; a separate low dot is visible.
- vertical marginal connector: `\unsure{\vert|\downarrow}` — The lower rounded loop/knob does not expose two distinct head arms.
- caption word: `les \unsure{supérieurs}` — Les is readable; the following p-like stroke and upper traces support supérieurs but the middle is merged.
- raised sign in cancelled fragment: `\deleted{H^{\unsure{i}}(X,}` — H is readable while the small raised mark is intersected by cancellation strokes.
- underlined adjective: `\uline{\unsure{affine|fini}}` — Two tall f-like peaks favor affine; the initial entry and low final run are compressed.
- cancelled initial before Ext: `\deleted{\unsure{\mathcal H|E}}` — The looped tall form is crossed and retraced, leaving its lower body partly obscured.
- raised Ext exponent: `\unsure{p|1|i}` — The shoulder/bowl and descending trace favor p; the dense upper stroke could also be a flagged1 or i.
- parenthesized Pic exponent: `\unsure{(q)|(1)}` — The filled upper-left bulb and long stroke remain too thick to resolve.
- cancelled partial abbreviation: `\deleted{\unsure{\operatorname{Ex}}}` — Only the E-like and x-like parts survive separately beneath the crossing strokes.
- opening delimiter of third case: `H^2\unsure{(}X,D(G))` — The opening curve cannot be detached confidently from the terminal stroke of the preceding H/2 group.
- third-case relation: `\mathrel{\substack{\unsure{\#|\neq}\\\hphantom{\#}!}}` — Two oblique crossings favor a hash shape, while one may be retracing of a one-stroke inequality.
- clipped upper group: `\operatorname{\underline{P}ic}^{\unsure{(2}}_X` — The physical right boundary removes the end of the raised group.
- lower first word: `\unsure{abélien}` — The ascenders and dot support the word, but the small letters are flattened.
- lower punctuation: `\unsure{,}` — The low mark is separated by a broad space and is not fully secure as punctuation.
- lower second word: `\unsure{alors|dans}` — The tall arch and low continuation fit both compressed cursive readings; alors stays preferred.
- isolated letter and local state: `\unsure{\mathcal A|\deleted{H}|H}` — The high arch favors A. The lower hooked stroke may be its flourish rather than a separate cancellation.
- lower Ext index: `\unsure{\mathcal A|q}` — The two descending limbs favor A but are thickly joined to the upper loop.
- bottom Pic exponent: `\unsure{q|1|?}` — The upper-left bulb and descending stem favor q, without an independent question-mark dot.
- bottom i=0 label relation: `\unsure{=}` — The very short label mark may merge two strokes and remains qualified.

Witness coverage: H is an actual transcription on this page. Every mathematical/textual block and agreed token was read. TikZ/array layout and editorial prose-description differences are not readings by themselves. H correctly describes the broad curve sketches, marginal placement, cancelled Hom and the presence of the next cancelled group, the separate exclamation, and the unfinished final equality; those features alone are not omissions. The exact second cancelled group now retains qualified Ex without a supplied t, and the i=2 relation is hash/inequality with a separate lower-right exclamation already reported by H. Its explicit raised/low f-sign, Pic-circle, attachment and vertical-pair claims remain evaluated locally. The added prose avec and descriptive wrappers are not literal source text. Systematic mathcal-E and mathbb-G wrappers remain editorial conventions; actual alphabet alternatives and partial underline spans are preserved independently. Both low G points, inserted R, qualified lower prose, bottom cases, separator and foliation remain accounted for. All thirteen original C marks have separate dispositions. Same-preference added uncertainty and narrowed doubt do not create C corrections. The lower-frame Ext and Pic exponents, lower Ext index and bottom Pic exponent retain their stated alternatives; the equality between bottom zeros is resolved, while its label relation remains qualified.
